We first identify sentences containing keywords listed in Appendix A, we then manually read sentences centering around the keyword sentences to identify GAI contextual sentences. GAI-related sentences include both the keyword sentences and the contextual sentences</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI% in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI% for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Pos</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI% if the net sentiment of GAI discussions is positive and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Pos</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Pos in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Pos for the same firm in the one-year of pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neg</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI% if the net sentiment of GAI discussions is negative and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Neg</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neg in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Neg for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI% if the net sentiment of GAI discussions is neutral and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Neu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neu in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Neu for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI% if the GAI discussion contains GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify whether a corpus of text contains initiative of adopting GAI. Our prompt is as follows: “Does the text involve real initiatives or plans in generative AI? Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-NonInitiative</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI% if the GAI discussion exists and does not contain GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify whether a corpus of text contains initiative of adopting GAI. Our prompt is as follows: “Does the text involve real initiatives or plans in generative AI? Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-NonInitiative</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-NonInitiative in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-NonInitiative for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Analysts’ GAI discussion intensity, measured as the percentage of GAI-related sentences in their questions in a conference call, multiplied by 100. We identify GAI-related sentences following a two-step procedure. We first identify sentences containing keywords listed in Appendix A. We then manually read sentences centering around the keyword sentences to identify GAI contextual sentences. GAI-related sentences include both the keyword sentences and the contextual sentences</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Table 14 (continued)</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Variable</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Definition</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year of pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Pos-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Analyst if the net sentiment of analysts&#x27; GAI discussions is positive and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Pos-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Pos-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Pos-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neg-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Analyst if the net sentiment of analysts&#x27; GAI discussions is negative and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Neg-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neg-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Neg-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neu-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Analyst if the net sentiment of analysts&#x27; GAI discussions is neutral and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Neu-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Neu-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Neu-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Analyst if analysts&#x27; GAI discussions contain GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify whether a corpus of text contains initiative of adopting GAI. Our prompt is as follows: &quot;Does the text involve real initiatives or plans in generative AI? Answer with 1 for &quot;Yes&quot;, 0 for &quot;No&quot;. Text: {}.&quot;</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-NonInitiative-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Analyst if analysts&#x27; GAI discussion exists and does not contain GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify whether a corpus of text contains initiative of adopting GAI. Our prompt is as follows: &quot;Does the text involve real initiatives or plans in generative AI? Answer with 1 for &quot;Yes&quot;, 0 for &quot;No&quot;. Text: {}.&quot;</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-NonInitiative-Analyst</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-NonInitiative-Analyst in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-NonInitiative-Analyst for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Pos-Broad</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals  $ \Delta $GAI%-Pos minus  $ \Delta $GAI%-Neg</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Already Happened)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion contains GAI-related initiatives that have already happened and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: &quot;Does the text discuss a real generative AI related action that already happened? Answer with 1 for &quot;Yes&quot;, 0 for &quot;No&quot;. Text: {}.&quot;</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Already Happened)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Already Happened) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Already Happened) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Specific)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion contains specific GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: &quot;Is the text that discusses real initiatives or plans in generative AI specific? Specificity includes mentioning a product, an application, a number, a function, or an example. Answer with 1 for &quot;Yes&quot;, 0 for &quot;No&quot;. Text: {}.&quot;</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Table 14 (continued)</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Variable</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Definition</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Specific)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Specific) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Specific) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Partner)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion contains GAI-related initiatives that relate to a specific business partner and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Is the text that discusses real initiatives or plans in generative AI specific? Specificity includes mentioning a product, an application, a number, a function, or an example. Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Partner)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Partner) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Partner) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Certain)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion of GAI-related initiatives is sufficiently certain and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Does the text that involves real initiatives or plans in generative AI discuss the initiatives or plans with sufficient certainty? Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Certain)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Certain) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Certain) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Already Happened)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion does not contain GAI-related initiatives that have already happened and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Does the text discuss a real generative AI related action that already happened? Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Not Already Happened)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Already Happened) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Not Already Happened) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Specific)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion does not contain specific GAI-related initiatives and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Is the text that discusses real initiatives or plans in generative AI specific? Specificity includes mentioning a product, an application, a number, a function, or an example. Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (Not Specific)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Specific) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Not Specific) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (No Partner)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion does not contain GAI-related initiatives that relate to a specific business partner and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Is the text that discusses real initiatives or plans in generative AI specific? Specificity includes mentioning a product, an application, a number, a function, or an example. Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta $GAI%-Initiative (No Partner)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (No Partner) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (No Partner) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Table 14 (continued)</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Variable</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Definition</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Certain)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Equals GAI%-Initiative if the GAI discussion of GAI-related initiatives is not sufficiently certain and zero otherwise. We use ChatGPT-4 to identify these initiatives. Our prompt is as follows: “Does the text that involves real initiatives or plans in generative AI discuss the initiatives or plans with sufficient certainty? Answer with 1 for “Yes”, 0 for “No”. Text: {}.”</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>$ \Delta GAI\%-Initiative $ (Not Certain)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GAI%-Initiative (Not Certain) in post-ChatGPT period minus the average GAI%-Initiative (Not Certain) for the same firm in the one-year pre-ChatGPT period</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Market Reaction Variables | Call CAR|</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Absolute cumulative abnormal returns over the two-day window [0, +1] around the conference call. Abnormal returns are calculated based on Fama-French five-factor model. We multiply it by 100 for the ease of interpretation</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Ab. Volume</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Excess trading volume over the two-day window [0, +1], measured as the two-day trading volume divided by number of shares outstanding, less the average two-day turnover in the estimation period [-120, -30], following Bushee et al. (2011). We multiply it by 100 for the ease of interpretation</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>GPT Launch CAR</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Abnormal return cumulated over a two-day window [0, +1], where day 0 is the ChatGPT launch date, November 30, 2022. We calculate abnormal return based on Fama-French five-factor model and multiply it by 100 for the ease of interpretation</td></tr><tr><td colspan="2">Independent Variables</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Abnormal Return Volatility</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Standard deviation of daily market-adjusted return over the past year</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Absolute Call Sentiment</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Percentage of sentiment words (Loughran and McDonald 2011) in the entire conference call transcript</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Absolute Earnings Surprise</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Absolute value of the difference between quarterly EPS and consensus analyst forecasts (median) in the latest 90 days, scaled by the stock price at the end of the latest fiscal quarter</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>AIIE</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Industry-level labor exposure to AI from Felten et al. (2021), calculated as a weighted average of the AI Occupational Exposure (AIOE) scores across all occupations within an industry. AIOE for an occupation is derived by assessing the relevance of specific AI applications to the abilities required in that occupation</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Analyst Coverage</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The natural logarithm of one plus the number of analysts who issue EPS forecasts for the firm over the past year</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Customer</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Indicator variable that equals one if the firm is in the retail or service industries and zero otherwise. We classify the retail industry by two-digit SIC codes 52-59 and the service industry by two-digit SIC codes 72, 73, and 78</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>CS Threat</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Textual similarity between 10-K cybersecurity threats related sentences and the disclosures of actual incidents of cyberattacks from the Privacy Rights Clearinghouse’s Data Breach Chronology Database. The firm’s cybersecurity threats related sentences are extracted from “Item 1a. Risk Factors” from its latest 10-K filing and identified as sentences containing keywords such as “cyberattack,” “data breaches,” and “data theft,” following Florackis et al. (2023). We next compile the descriptions of cyberattack incidents on PRC database and calculate its cosine similarity with the firm’s cybersecurity threats related sentences</td></tr></table>

<div style="text-align: center;"><div style="text-align: center;">Table 14 (continued)</div> </div>




<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Variable</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Definition</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Firm Age</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Number of years the firm shows up in Compustat. We divide it by 10 for the ease of interpretation</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>High Tech Industry</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Indicator variable that equals one if two-digit sic codes belong to 28, 35, 36, 73, and 87 and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Institutional Shareholding</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Percentage of shares outstanding owned by institutional investors, at the end of the latest fiscal quarter</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Market to Book</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Market value of equity, at the latest fiscal year-end divided by book value of equity</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Miss R&amp;D</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Indicator variable that equals one when Compustat variable (xrd) is missing and zero otherwise</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Patents</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The total number of patents filed over the past three calendar years. To overcome the skewness, we take inverse hyperbolic sine transformation, following Glaeser (2018)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quarterly Market to Book</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Market value at the end of the latest fiscal quarter divided by book value</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quarterly Market Value</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Logarithm of market value at the end of the latest fiscal quarter</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quarterly Return on Asset</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quarterly net income over total assets for the latest fiscal quarter</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Quarterly Return Volatility</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Return volatility over the period of  $ [-127, -2] $ days relative to the conference call date (i.e., day 0) (Mayew and Venkatachalam 2012)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>R&amp;D</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>R&amp;D expenditure over total assets for the latest fiscal quarter</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Size</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>The natural logarithm of total assets, at the latest fiscal quarter-end</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Trade Secret</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>An indicator equals one if the firm&#x27;s past three year 10-K filings mention “trade secret” or “trade secrecy” and zero otherwise</td></tr></table>
